Vere 4.6 failed standalone fake-pier startup, trial 4, Linux ARM64.
Sanitization: replaced fixture/test roots, operator home prefixes and host labels with placeholders.
Syscall order, PIDs, descriptors, addresses and lengths are unchanged.
The top-level LMDB mapping is unmapped before the epoch mapping; both are retained below.
Normal demand-paging SIGSEGV events within the loom are distinct from the external 0x10 cleanup fault.
